On multiple-choice GPQA-Diamond, the per-case answer preference explains 81-93% of the held-out test-run agreement index: the shared-bias-dominates account over-claims here, because a wrong but attractive option the whole cohort latches onto is captured by the per-case preference channel (whether that preference is induced by shared training bias is not identified). On open-domain AIME, the mechanical preference explains only 59-78% (21-29% if shrunk to pure noise), and a preference-unexplained residual of 1.56-2.80 Gamma units survives, which a run-level preference-heterogeneity reference more than absorbs (1.4-2.1). A self-consistency backfire on hard questions is reproduced (binned voting gap down to -0.09, coupled CI [-0.12,-0.07]), and the highest-agreement bin reaches an accuracy of only 0.42-0.83, a 1.2-3.6x lift over base rate: agreement is graded evidence, not certification. No new voting method is proposed; code and evidence are committed and reproducible.

</details>


### [139] [Do Large Language Models Hallucinate Electric Fata Morganas?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18816)
*Kristina Šekrst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文认为AI幻觉不仅是工程缺陷，还具有关于机器意识的哲学意义。通过两项实证研究（GPT模型的温度参数实验和编码器模型对比实验），作者论证了让模型看起来"有创造力"的采样参数恰恰会增加其幻觉率，且幻觉源于训练数据的多样性和主观性，而非认知能力的发展。文章进一步主张，模型对情感或知觉的自我报告符合幻觉的定义，未来机器意识可能因与高级幻觉难以区分而在认知上不可及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上AI幻觉被视为需要解决的工程缺陷，但作者认为它在机器意识问题上有重要的哲学意涵，值得从认识论和心智哲学的角度重新审视。

Method: 采用理论分析与实证研究相结合的方法：(1) 梳理LLM幻觉的已知成因（源目标发散、训练与推理不一致、过拟合）；(2) 第一项实验：在不同温度下对GPT系列模型提出模糊事实问题，比较答案差异；(3) 第二项实验：测试基于百科数据训练的编码器模型对同类问题的回答；(4) 引用图灵、塞尔中文屋、框架问题及维纳和阿什比的控制论传统进行哲学论证。

Result: 高温采样产生看似合理但错误的答案，低温产生事实准确的答案；使模型看似有创造性和自发性的采样参数与提高幻觉率的参数相同。编码器模型的回答事实准确且无修饰，表明幻觉源于主观和社会多样性训练数据，而非认知能力的发展。模型对情感或知觉的自我报告符合幻觉的定义。

Conclusion: 机器意识若未来出现，可能仍然在认识论上不可及，因为它与足够先进的幻觉无法区分。这一结论对图灵测试的有效性和机器意识的可验证性提出了根本性挑战。

Abstract: AI hallucinations - that is, outputs which are made up, cannot be verified, or contradict the source material - are generally regarded as an engineering flaw to be dealt with. This paper contends that they also have philosophical significance when it comes to the question of machine consciousness. We examine the known causes of hallucinations in large language models - such as source-target divergence, discrepancies between training and inference, and overfitting - and we present two empirical investigations. In the first, we apply successive generations of the GPT model to ambiguous factual questions under different temperature settings, finding that higher temperatures result in plausible but incorrect answers while lower temperatures lead to factually accurate ones. The sampling parameters that cause a model to seem creative or spontaneous and thus more likely to pass behavioral tests of intelligence are the same ones that increase its hallucination rate. In the second, we look at an encoder-only model that has been trained on encyclopedic data and which answers questions of the same type factually and without embellishment, indicating that hallucinations are due to exposure to subjective and socially diverse training data rather than to the development of any cognitive ability. Using references to Turing, Searle's Chinese Room, the frame problem, and the cybernetic tradition of Wiener and Ashby, we claim that a model's self-reports of emotion or sentience come within the definition of hallucination, and that any future occurrence of machine consciousness might remain epistemically inaccessible since it would be indistinguishable from a sufficiently advanced hallucination.

</details>


### [140] [Identifying Implicit Premises for Logical Reconstruction of Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18821)
*Xuyao Feng,Anthony Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号管道，利用大语言模型生成隐含前提，并将其转换为逻辑公式，以证明文本中陈述对之间的逻辑关系（蕴含、矛盾或中立），在Microtext论证语料库上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言文本中重建论证图面临隐前提（enthymemes）的挑战。现有方法要么是用NLP识别文本中的隐前提，要么是用基于溯因的符号方法在逻辑表示中寻找缺失前提，但缺乏能生成隐前提来逻辑证明已知陈述对之间蕴含或矛盾关系的方法。

Method: 提出一个神经符号管道：首先使用大语言模型（LLMs）生成中间隐含前提，然后将这些前提翻译为逻辑公式，并与表示显式前提和显式主张的逻辑公式结合，用以展示它们之间的逻辑关系（蕴含、矛盾或中立）。

Result: 该方法在Microtext Argumentative Corpus上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该神经符号方法结合了LLM生成隐前提的能力与符号逻辑推理的优势，能够有效生成隐含前提以证明陈述对之间的逻辑关系。

Abstract: The logical reconstruction of argument graphs from natural language text is challenging because of the prevalence of enthymemes (i.e., arguments with implicit premises). There are natural language processing methods for identifying enthymemes in text, and there are symbolic methods based on abduction for identifying missing premises in a logical representation of enthymemes. However, there is a need for methods to generate implicit premises to logically show a known entailment or contradiction relationship between a pair of statements. To address this, we propose a neuro-symbolic pipeline that uses large language models (LLMs) to generate intermediate implicit premises that are translated into logical formulae and used with logical formulae representing explicit premises and explicit claims to show the logical relationships between them (entailment, contradiction, or neutrality). Our approach is evaluated on the Microtext Argumentative Corpus.

</details>


### [141] [Understanding Multilingual Medical ASR Adaptation Through Layer-Wise Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18825)
*Souranil Kahali,Rituparna Bose,Abner Hernandez,Tomas Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Ning Ma,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过逐层编码器分析，探究多语言医学微调如何重塑Whisper模型的内部表示，比较了多种适应策略（零样本、仅英语、仅德语、两阶段连续微调和直接英德联合微调）在不同模型规模下的表现与内部表征变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学自动语音识别（MedASR）需要适应专业术语、有限的临床标注数据和多语言场景。虽然Whisper等大规模预训练ASR模型泛化能力强，但医学和多语言适应后其内部行为如何变化，仅靠词错误率（WER）无法充分理解，需要深入分析内部表示层面的变化。

Method: 采用逐层编码器分析方法，对比五种适应设置：零样本解码、仅英语微调、仅德语诊断微调、两阶段EN→EN+DE连续微调、直接EN+DE联合微调，在多个Whisper模型规模下进行实验，并追踪两阶段Whisper-Small的训练轨迹进行逐层表示分析。

Result: 微调显著提升MedASR性能，但最佳模型取决于适应设置：Whisper-Medium在直接英德联合训练下英语WER最低（7.72%）且综合WER最低（26.30%）；仅德语的Whisper-Large-v3德语WER最低（44.96%），但这是基于86条单说话者训练话语的语料内诊断，而非稳健泛化。逐层分析表明英语医学微调主导编码器变化，多语言连续微调基本保持已适应的表示空间；领域和语言信息在各层高度可恢复，而线性可恢复的错误预测线索随WER改善而减弱。

Conclusion: 医学微调对Whisper的内部表示改造主要源于英语医学适应，多语言延续学习较少扰动已适应的表示空间；模型性能提升与内部错误预测信息减弱相关联，提示需要更精细的适应策略设计以平衡多语言MedASR的泛化能力。

Abstract: Medical automatic speech recognition (MedASR) requires adaptation to specialised terminology, limited annotated clinical data, and multilingual use cases. Although large-scale pretrained ASR models such as Whisper achieve strong generalisation, their behaviour after medical and multilingual adaptation remains insufficiently understood beyond word error rate (WER). This paper investigates how multilingual medical adaptation reshapes the internal representations of Whisper models through layer-wise encoder analysis. We compare zero-shot decoding, English-only fine-tuning, German-only diagnostic fine-tuning, two-stage EN->EN+DE continuation, and direct EN+DE fine-tuning across Whisper model sizes. Fine-tuning substantially improves MedASR performance, but the best model depends on the adaptation setting: Whisper-Medium gives the lowest English WER (7.72%) and the lowest combined EN+DE WER under direct EN+DE training (26.30%); German-only Whisper-Large-v3 gives the lowest German WER (44.96%), but as a within-corpus diagnostic on 86 single-speaker training utterances rather than robust generalisation. Layer-wise analysis of the two-stage Whisper-Small trajectory shows that English medical fine-tuning produces the dominant encoder shift, whereas multilingual continuation largely preserves the adapted representation space. Domain and language information remain highly recoverable across layers, while linearly recoverable error-predictive cues weaken as WER improves.

</details>


### [142] [Assessing Quality of Experience in Natural Language Generation of German Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18888)
*Dinh Nam Pham,Shushen Manakhimova,Vivien Macketanz,Sebastian Möller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TextQ-German，一个面向德语自然语言生成（NLG）的人类中心评价数据集，从体验质量（QoE）角度覆盖自动文本摘要和机器翻译任务。通过众包研究收集人类质量评分，并开发了包括基于Transformer、语言特征和混合方法的自动QoE预测模型，其中混合模型在几乎所有实验设置中优于纯Transformer基线，为德语NLG评价提供了公开资源和基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）等NLG系统在真实世界中的广泛部署，传统的自动评价指标无法捕捉感知质量的多维特性，因此需要从用户角度出发、基于人类感知质量的评价体系。

Method: 构建TextQ-German数据集，通过德语母语者的众包研究收集人类质量评分，识别各任务相关的感知质量维度；开发三种自动QoE预测模型（Transformer基、语言特征基和混合方法）；并用LLM生成输出扩展数据集并标注总体QoE分数。

Result: 混合模型在几乎所有实验设置中优于纯Transformer基线；仅使用语言特征即可接近微调语言模型的性能；在保留集上的最终验证表明模型能够泛化到未见数据。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了面向NLG评价的公开可用资源和自动QoE预测基线，为开发更符合人类质量感知的NLG系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Natural Language Generation (NLG) has made the reliable evaluation of generated text increasingly critical, as these systems, such as large language models (LLMs), are now widely deployed in real-world applications. However, traditional automatic metrics fail to capture the multifaceted nature of perceived quality. In this paper, we introduce TextQ-German, a novel dataset suite for human-centered evaluation of German NLG from a Quality of Experience (QoE) perspective, covering automatic text summarization and machine translation. Through crowdsourcing studies with German speakers, we collect human quality ratings and identify relevant perceptual quality dimensions for each task. We develop automatic QoE prediction models, including transformer-based, linguistic feature-based, and hybrid approaches. Hybrid models outperform pure transformer baselines in almost all experimental settings, while linguistic features alone can approach the performance of fine-tuned language models. The dataset is extended with LLM-generated outputs annotated with overall QoE scores. Final validation on held-out sets indicates generalization to unseen data. Our work contributes a publicly accessible resource for NLG evaluation and baselines for automatic QoE prediction, providing a foundation for developing NLG systems that better align with human quality perception.

</details>


### [143] [SMTrap: Cost-Effective DoS Attacks Against Large Reasoning Models via SMT Conflict Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18921)
*Jian Yang,Zhenqi Feng,Zhaoyang Yu,Zhaoxin Fan,Kejian Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jianjun Huang,Wei You,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SMTrap，一种无需模型反馈的LRM-DoS攻击框架，利用SMT求解器的冲突计数作为外部信号，引导生成推理密集的CSP实例，从而在不查询目标模型、不训练攻击模型、不需要GPU的情况下实现多倍于现有基线的拒绝服务效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM-DoS方法严重依赖模型反馈（反复查询目标模型或训练专门攻击模型），这些昂贵操作削弱了攻击的杠杆效应。需要一种新的范式来降低成本、提升攻击效率。

Method: 提出"搜索放大"范式：利用SMT求解器产生的冲突计数作为低成本外部信号，指导构造推理密集的CSP实例。核心观察是LRM求解CSP时依赖试错回溯搜索，SMT冲突计数越高，LRM回溯搜索越广泛、输出轨迹越长。基于此构建SMTrap框架，仅需CPU即可运行。

Result: 在七个前沿模型上的评估表明，SMTrap实现了SOTA的LRM-DoS能力，产生的DoS效果比现有基线强数倍。同时展示了一种基于工具（tool-based）的缓解方案，可显著降低token使用量。

Conclusion: SMTrap验证了利用外部求解器信号代替模型反馈来生成高效DoS攻击的可行性，并为该威胁提供了有效的缓解途径，为LRM安全性研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Existing LRM-DoS methods rely heavily on model feedback to synthesize attack queries, requiring either repeated queries to the target model or training a dedicated attack model. These expensive operations severely weaken attack leverage. In this paper, we propose \emph{search amplification}, a novel, model-feedback-free LRM-DoS paradigm. It employs the conflict count derived from an Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solver as a low-cost external signal to guide the synthesis of inference-heavy Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP) instances. Our key observation is that LRMs depend on trial-and-backtracking search when solving CSPs, where higher SMT conflict counts on a given CSP instance positively correlate with more extensive LRM backtracking search and substantially longer output trajectories. Building on this finding, we propose \textsc{SMTrap}, a lightweight, CPU-only framework. Guided by SMT conflict counts, \textsc{SMTrap} generates inference-heavy CSP queries without model queries, attack-model training, or GPU computation. Evaluations across seven frontier models demonstrate the state-of-the-art LRM-DoS capability of \textsc{SMTrap}, producing DoS effects multiple times stronger than existing baselines. To mitigate the threat of \textsc{SMTrap}, we demonstrate a tool-based mitigation that significantly cuts token usage.

</details>


### [144] [Test-Time Scaling in the Wild: Why Exploitation, Not Exploration, Is the Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18931)
*Davide Romano,Kanak Raj,Jerrod Parker,Daniele Giofrè*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在五个开放式生成基准（医学、法律、金融、通用对话、创意写作）上对五种测试时扩展（TTS）方法进行计算标准化比较，发现扩展探索（候选池）有效，但利用（从候选池中选择最终输出）环节失效——奖励模型与真实质量相关性仅约0.12，选择近乎随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（TTS）在数学和代码任务上效果显著，但此前几乎只在验证容易的任务上开发和测试。开放式生成任务中验证困难，TTS方法是否仍然有效尚未可知，因此需要一个统一的框架来评估不同TTS家族在开放式生成中的表现。

Method: 作者提出了一个统一框架，将每种方法的token预算分解为探索（exploration）和利用（exploitation）两个维度。对五种TTS家族（多候选生成、部分序列搜索、迭代精炼、树搜索、候选融合）进行计算标准化的对比评估，涵盖五个开放式生成基准。

Result: 关键发现包括：扩展探索有效（候选池中最佳候选随计算量稳定提升）；但利用环节崩溃（奖励模型相关性仅ρ≈0.12，选择近乎随机）；树搜索因多样性崩溃加剧失败；精炼仅在5个基准中的1个有效，其他效果被混杂因素干扰；只有候选融合（Fusion）持续优于单样本基线，但仍仅恢复约40%的可用质量。

Conclusion: 开放式生成中TTS的瓶颈不在候选池的生成，而在于如何从中选择最终输出。当前奖励模型无法可靠区分候选质量，导致利用环节失效，这为未来TTS研究指明了方向——需要更好的选择机制而非更多的候选生成。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model outputs by spending additional inference compute - generating multiple candidates, searching over partial sequences, or iteratively refining drafts. These techniques yield large gains on mathematics and code, but have been developed and stress-tested almost exclusively on tasks where verification is straightforward. We conduct the first compute-normalised comparison of five TTS families across five open-ended generation benchmarks spanning medicine, law, finance, general chat, and creative writing - grounded in a unified framework that decomposes the effectiveness of each method's token budget into exploration and exploitation. The answer depends on which side of that decomposition you examine. Scaling exploration works: the best candidate in the pool improves steadily with compute across all settings. What breaks is exploitation - the step that converts a rich candidate pool into a final output. With state-of-the-art generators, reward models correlate at only $ρ_v \approx 0.12$ with true quality, rendering selection near-random regardless of budget. Tree search amplifies this failure through diversity collapse. Refinement helps on one of five benchmarks; its apparent gains elsewhere are confounded. Only synthesis across candidates (Fusion) consistently improves over single-sample baselines, yet still recovers only ~40% of available quality. The candidate pool is not the bottleneck - choosing from it is.

</details>


### [145] [MedUAG: Unified Understanding and Generation for Medical Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18937)
*Zijie Meng,Yuncheng Zhang,Hualiang Wang,Yitian Tang,Xiaotang Gai,Chen Shen,Songtao Jiang,Shaosheng Cao,Jian Wu,Xian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了医疗领域统一理解与生成（UAG）框架的全面基础，包括最大的医疗UAG数据集MedUAGCorpus（超600万实例、14种成像模态）、系统化基准MedUAGBench（12项多样化生成任务），以及端到端训练的统一医疗模型MedUAG，实验验证其在理解和生成任务上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正快速向统一理解与生成（UAG）框架演进，但将该范式拓展到医疗领域面临两大障碍：缺乏全面的训练与评估基准，以及缺乏经过广泛验证的统一医疗模型。

Method: 论文从三个方面构建医疗UAG基础：（1）构建MedUAGCorpus数据集，涵盖超600万实例、14种成像模态，是目前最大的医疗统一理解和生成数据集；（2）提出MedUAGBench基准，在标准化协议下将医疗生成评估扩展到12个多样化任务；（3）开发MedUAG模型，采用端到端训练方式，整合上述资源和统一框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，MedUAG在广泛的理解和生成任务上均取得强性能，建立了具有竞争力的基线。

Conclusion: MedUAG为下一代医疗多模态系统奠定了坚实基础，填补了医疗领域UAG研究在数据和模型方面的空白，为后续研究提供了基准参考。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving into unified understanding and generation (UAG) frameworks. However, extending these unified paradigms to the medical domain is hindered by: the absence of comprehensive training and evaluation benchmarks, and the lack of broadly validated unified medical model. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive foundation for medical UAG. First, we construct MedUAGCorpus, the largest unified medical understanding and generation dataset to date, comprising over 6 million instances across 14 imaging modalities. Second, we introduce MedUAGBench, a systematic benchmark that expands medical generation evaluation to 12 diverse tasks under standardized protocols. Finally, leveraging these resources, we develop MedUAG, an end-to-end trained unified medical model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedUAG achieves strong performance across a wide array of understanding and generation tasks, establishing a competitive baseline and paving the way for next-generation medical multimodal systems.

</details>


### [146] [Institutional Newspapers Pipeline: Deriving billions of high quality tokens from historical newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18972)
*Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Eben English,Jake Sadow,Kacie Bailey,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jessica Chapel,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与波士顿公共图书馆联合设计的模块化报纸处理流水线（Institutional Newspapers Pipeline），从历史报纸扫描件中提取高质量结构化数据，并在147万余张扫描件上运行，产出163亿个token和8310万个独立裁剪区域，同时发布开源流水线、模型和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史报纸是公共生活的重要记录，但其版面密集、不规则且带有噪声，导致计算化访问面临挑战和局限，需要一种高效且可解释的自动化方案来提取高质量结构化数据。

Method: 设计了一个模块化流水线，每个步骤可解释且可定制，计算开销足以在工作站级硬件上运行。流程包括：扫描件分割为类型无关的裁剪区域、逐区域OCR、文本分析、类型分类、阅读顺序检测、命名实体识别、主题分类、语言检测以及预计算嵌入生成。

Result: 流水线在波士顿公共图书馆的部分馆藏上运行，覆盖1795至1930年间1,473,635张公共领域报纸扫描件，OCR输出达163亿个token，共8310万个裁剪区域。论文还报告了各步骤的评估结果和数据集规模测量数据。

Conclusion: 这项工作为从数千万张报纸扫描件中解锁高质量数据迈出了实质性一步，通过开源流水线、模型和数据集，为历史报纸的数字化研究提供了可复用的基础设施。

Abstract: Historical newspapers are an abundant record of public life, but their dense, irregular and sometimes noisy layouts make computational access to these materials both challenging and limited. We present the Institutional Newspapers Pipeline, a modular system we jointly designed with Boston Public Library to extract high-quality, structured datasets from historical newspaper scans. It was architected so that each step remains interpretable and customizable, and so that the pipeline as a whole remains computationally frugal enough to run on workstation-level hardware. The pipeline runs each scan through a multi-step process: it segments scans into individual type-agnostic crops and performs OCR on each resulting segment before then performing text analysis, type classification, reading order detection, named entities recognition, subject classification, language detection, and pre-computed embeddings generation on every crop. We ran this pipeline against a portion of Boston Public Library's holdings and released the results as an open dataset. The optical character recognition (OCR) output represents 16.3 billion o200k_base tokens across 83.1 million individual crops, extracted from 1,473,635 public domain newspaper scans published between 1795 and 1930. This report describes our methods for each processing step, the small models we trained, as well as the evaluation results and dataset-scale measurements we collected in the process. It accompanies the release of the pipeline, models, and dataset. We position this work as a substantial step towards unlocking high-quality data from tens of millions of newspaper scans.

</details>


### [147] [DeepWeaver: Bridging the Evidence Synthesis Gap in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18988)
*Xujia Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepWeaver框架，通过维护Thought Block Chains（TBCs）这一结构化表示来弥合检索与生成之间的"证据综合鸿沟"，并在提出的LoQA基准及DeepResearch Bench上验证了其在内容充分性、引文质量等方面优于直接生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索-生成流水线在开放域问答中普遍使用，但仅靠检索不够——LLM需将嘈杂且零散的证据组织成全面且引文规范的答案。直接生成往往低效利用证据、引文错位、信息坍缩为浅层摘要，存在"证据综合鸿沟"。

Method: 提出DeepWeaver框架，通过维护Thought Block Chains（TBCs）——一种将主张、关键信息、关键词和支持证据分组的结构化表示来编织嘈杂的检索证据。使用下级TBCs检查残余证据、提交TBC修订并发现新主张，最终生成综合答案。

Result: 在知识库和网页两类开放域QA场景中评估，并引入高密度证据综合基准LoQA。在多个LLM上，DeepWeaver在LoQA上提升了内容充分性、引文接地性和细节保留，在DeepResearch Bench上实现了更深洞察和更高引文质量。

Conclusion: 证据编织是弥合开放域QA中检索与生成之间鸿沟的有效机制，DeepWeaver为该问题提供了可行且可复现的方案。

Abstract: Retrieve-then-generate pipelines are commonly used to produce deep-research answers for open-ended questions, but retrieval alone is insufficient: LLMs must organize noisy and fragmented evidence into comprehensive, well-cited answers. We refer to this process as evidence synthesis. However, direct generation often underuses evidence, misaligns citations, and collapses diverse information into shallow summaries, exposing an evidence synthesis gap between retrieval and generation. Thus, we propose DeepWeaver, a novel framework that weaves noisy retrieved evidence into comprehensive answers by maintaining Thought Block Chains (TBCs), a structured representation that groups claims, salient information, keywords, and supporting evidence. DeepWeaver uses subordinate TBCs to inspect residual evidence, commit TBC revisions, and discover new claims before final generation. We evaluate DeepWeaver on open-ended QA over both knowledge bases and the web, and introduce LoQA, a high-density benchmark for evidence synthesis. Across multiple LLMs, DeepWeaver improves content sufficiency, citation grounding, and detail preservation on LoQA, while achieving deeper insights and higher citation quality on DeepResearch Bench. These results show that evidence weaving is an effective mechanism for bridging retrieval and generation in open-ended QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/KlozeWang/DeepWeaver.

</details>


### [148] [Structure, Association, and Decision Value: Representation-Based Difficulty Estimation for Adaptive Inference in African-Language NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19003)
*Toheeb Ogunade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在15种非洲语言的自然语言推理任务中，内部表示统计量能否为自适应推理提供有用的样本级难度信号，结果表明不能。作者发现AfriXNLI数据集存在与XNLI测试集的严重重叠问题，参数规模不能可靠排序非洲语言能力，表示统计量与计算效益目标之间的关联因目标而异且很弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非洲多语言NLP场景中，是否可以利用模型内部表示统计量作为自适应推理的样本难度信号，以避免对所有样本都使用昂贵的推理计算，从而在计算预算受限时提高效率。

Method: 使用冻结的现成检查点，在15种非洲语言的NLI任务上进行实验。评估参数规模对能力排序的影响；在三个多语言表示空间中比较角度离散度和有效秩两个统计量；分别检验这两个信号与"升级后概率增益"和"升级是否改变预测"两个不同目标之间的关联，并分别在控制语言和未控制语言条件下进行相关分析。

Result: 四个主要发现：(1) AfriXNLI英语配置与XNLI测试数据大量重叠（1047/1050），一个NLI检查点在该测试分割上得1.000分，存在数据暴露问题；(2) 更大的参数规模在7种语言上更好、8种语言上更差，无显著总体差异；(3) 角度离散度比有效秩更多地由语言决定，合并相关性会夸大一个而掩盖另一个；(4) 有效秩预测概率增益但不能预测预测是否改变，便宜的模型置信度则相反，两目标仅0.655相关。没有信号能使自适应路由优于始终昂贵推理，但oracle在60%计算下超出11个准确率点。

Conclusion: 核心方法论发现是：一个表示统计量可能对某一种"计算收益"定义具有统计显著性，但对另一种无关，因此它是一个糟糕的决策变量。在测试条件下，自适应推理不值得采用，但存在11个准确率点的理论上限，未来值得探索更好的信号。

Abstract: We ask whether internal representation statistics can provide useful example-level difficulty signals for adaptive inference in multilingual African NLP, and find that they cannot in this setting. Studying natural language inference across 15 African languages with frozen off-the-shelf checkpoints, we report four results. First, AfriXNLI's English configuration shares 1,047 of its 1,050 examples verbatim with XNLI evaluation data, and one widely used NLI checkpoint scores 1.000 on that test split, consistent with XNLI test exposure. Because AfriXNLI is derived from XNLI, its English, French and Swahili configurations cannot serve as clean evaluations for XNLI-trained models. Second, parameter count does not reliably order capability across African languages: our larger checkpoint is better in seven languages and worse in eight, with no significant aggregate difference. Third, across three multilingual representation spaces, angular dispersion is consistently more language-determined than effective rank, so pooled correlations can inflate one and mask the other. Fourth, the association that survives language control depends on the target: effective rank predicts probability gain from escalation but not whether escalation changes the prediction, while cheap-model confidence shows the opposite pattern; the two targets correlate at only 0.655. Under the tested models, signals, and compute budgets, no evaluated signal makes adaptive routing preferable to always-expensive inference, although an oracle exceeds it by 11 accuracy points at 60% of the compute. Our central methodological finding is that a representation statistic can be statistically significant for one notion of computational benefit while being irrelevant to another, and therefore be a poor decision variable.

</details>


### [149] [Introducing the Privacy-HSD Trade-off: Hate Speech Detection, but not at the Cost of Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19006)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Vlad Garbuz,Chirill Donos,Maxim Dnestreanschii,Gabriel Creanga,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Thomas Lampert,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了仇恨言论检测（HSD）系统与用户隐私之间的冲突，发现HSD系统可能以编码作者身份为代价提升性能，从而威胁隐私。作者提出了"隐私-HSD权衡"概念，并基准测试了一系列文本私有化方法及新提出的领域专用AgnoSpeech技术，证明平衡隐私与HSD虽有难度但可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论对在线用户尤其是青少年和少数群体构成真实威胁，构建可靠的自动检测系统至关重要；但作者指出这必须与个人隐私权相平衡，因为现有HSD系统可能在提升性能的同时无意中编码作者身份信息，威胁用户隐私。

Method: 首先通过实验证明HSD系统在性能与作者身份编码之间存在关联，建立隐私-HSD权衡的概念框架；随后基准测试一系列文本私有化方法，并提出领域专用的AgnoSpeech技术以尝试实现两者的平衡。

Result: 结果表明平衡隐私与仇恨言论检测是困难的，但确实是可行的；AgnoSpeech等方法的对比测试为该问题提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 研究为隐私与HSD之间权衡的进一步研究提供了有力论据，两者对保障在线参与的持续健康都具有切实影响。

Abstract: Hate speech is a real and timely threat that affects a large portion of online users, especially youth and minority groups. While building reliable and robust automatic hate speech detection (HSD) systems is paramount, we argue that this must also be balanced with the individual right to privacy. Exploring the intersection of HSD and privacy, we demonstrate that HSD systems might unintentionally achieve performance at the cost of encoding authorship, posing a threat to privacy. Building on these findings, we establish the notion of a privacy-HSD trade-off, which demands a careful balance. We benchmark a series of text privatization methods, as well as our newly proposed domain-specific AgnoSpeech technique, showing that balancing privacy and HSD is difficult but feasible. The findings make a strong case for more research on the trade-offs between privacy and HSD, both of which have tangible implications for the safeguarding of online participation.

</details>


### [150] [Grading the Graders: Verification Autonomy Levels (L0-L5) for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19009)
*Yajie Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元标准"验证自主级别"(VAL)，将LLM验证方案沿单一轴分类——验证规格从何而来以及裁决保证什么，从L0（LLM自我声明）到L3/L4（可判定系统的完备性），并指出L5在非受限情况下不可能实现。文章揭示了文献中存在的"完备性盲区"以及完备性仅对形式化可规范属性可达的核心二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地与验证器配对使用，但验证文献中"level"一词至少被用来指代五种不同事物（验证粒度、概念抽象、风险层级、系统栈层、真值来源），造成系统性混淆。现有文献缺乏一个统一的验证分类标准，导致17篇论文中概念混用，亟需澄清。

Method: 作者提出验证自主级别(VAL)元标准，沿单一维度分类验证方案：验证规格来源及裁决保证。VAL从L0（无确定性锚点的LLM自我声明）经L2（客观真值、仅正确性）到L3/L4（具有单属性或域级完备性的可判定系统），L5在非受限情况下不可达。文章剖析了"完备性盲区"：基于替换和采样的验证器能确认候选成立，但无法证明无候选被遗漏。同时论证了一个二分法：完备性仅对形式化可规范的属性可达，而经验性开放世界验证（事实核查、诊断）只能达到锚定正确性(L2)。

Result: 研究在四个领域（符号数学、行为监控、医学诊断、代码生成）和最强现有形式验证基线中验证了VAL框架。结果表明验证粒度的层级、概念层级、风险和系统栈均与VAL正交，解决了17篇调研论文中的系统性混淆。L3/L4完备性仅适用于形式化可判定系统，开放世界验证止步于L2。

Conclusion: 验证自主级别(VAL)为LLM验证方案提供了清晰的统一分类标准，指出了完备性的可达边界：完备性仅对形式化可规范属性可达，经验性开放世界验证无法超越锚定正确性。该框架厘清了文献中长期混用的多个"level"概念，为未来验证系统的设计、评估和比较提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly paired with verifiers (step checkers, self-consistency filters, tool-based fact checkers, formal proof assistants) that claim to detect the model's errors. Yet the verification literature uses the word "level" to mean at least five different things: verification granularity, concept abstraction, risk tier, system-stack layer, and the epistemic source of the ground truth. We propose Verification Autonomy Levels (VAL), a meta-standard classifying verification schemes along a single axis: where does the verification spec come from, and what does the verdict guarantee? VAL ranges from L0 (LLM self-declaration, no deterministic anchor) through L2 (objective ground truth, correctness only) to L3/L4 (decidable systems with single-property or domain-level completeness), with L5 impossible in the unrestricted case. Central to VAL is the completeness blind spot: substitution- and sampling-based verifiers can confirm that proposed candidates hold, but cannot prove that no candidate was missed. We further identify a dichotomy the literature has not stated: completeness is reachable only for formally specifiable properties, while empirical open-world verification (fact-checking, diagnosis) caps at anchored correctness (L2). We document this across four domains (symbolic mathematics, behavior monitoring, medical diagnosis, and code generation) and in the strongest existing formal-verification baseline, whose authors note the verifier "focuses on the correctness of each step." We show the levels of granularity, concept hierarchy, risk, and system stack are orthogonal to VAL, resolving a systematic conflation across 17 surveyed papers. Code and full assessment are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [151] [Institutional Books - Enriched Text: A customizable multilingual open-source pipeline for denoising, deduplicating, and annotating OCR text at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19026)
*David Lowry-Duda,Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了机构图书：哈佛图书馆（IB-HL）数据集及其"富文本"（Enriched Text）处理方法。该数据集包含983,004卷、242B tokens的书籍，最初通过哈佛图书馆参与Google Books图书馆项目数字化而来。作者提出了富文本方法，通过保留元数据的注释来规范化文本，而非生成单一"完整"的token流，使用户能够根据自身需求定制输出。发布内容包括IB-HL-ET（含217B tokens、983,003卷、1.39B个带注释的子主题段落）及生成该数据集的流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模预处理流水线主要针对网络文本优化，倾向于激进过滤、去重、限制语言并丢弃元数据，这与IB-HL数据集的信息管理目标相冲突。同时，研究人员使用IB-HL时重复进行相似的预处理工作，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出"富文本"（Enriched Text）方法：不是生成单一完整的token流，而是规范化文本同时通过注释保留元数据。具体包括：分离后记（endmatter）、检测逐段落语言、识别重复段落簇、计算逐段落每字节位数（bits-per-byte）分数。所有这些信息通过类HTML注释层叠在文本之上提供，用户可解析注释自行定制输出，而非接受全局编辑决策。

Result: 发布IB-HL-ET数据集，包含217B o200k_base tokens、983,003卷、1.39B个带注释的子主题段落，以及生成该数据集的完整流水线。该流水线适用于集合中约250种语言。

Conclusion: 富文本方法使大型书籍集合更容易被机器解析、也更容易被人类研究，通过保留元数据注释而非删除，赋予用户更大的内容定制灵活性，解决了大规模预处理的标准化实践与细致信息管理目标之间的张力。

Abstract: Released in 2025, Institutional Books: Harvard Library (IB-HL) is a collection of 983,004 volumes (242B o200k_base tokens), originally digitized through Harvard Library's participation in the Google Books Library project. As researchers and developers have begun to use IB-HL, a tension has emerged between standard large-scale preprocessing practices and the goals of careful information stewardship. Many existing pipelines optimize for web text: as a result, they tend to aggressively filter, deduplicate, restrict by language, and sometimes discard meaningful metadata. Meanwhile, researchers seeking to use IB-HL duplicate effort while performing similar processing and analysis.
  We describe an approach that we call Enriched Text. Instead of producing a single 'complete' stream of tokens, we normalize the text while preserving metadata through annotations. We separate endmatter, detect per-paragraph language, identify clusters of duplicate paragraphs, and compute per-paragraph bits-per-byte scores. We provide this information through HTML-like annotations layered on top of the text. By parsing these annotations, users can tailor the output to their own needs instead of accepting a global editorial decision on content. The pipeline applies to all $\approx$250 languages in the collection.
  This report describes this project's goals, implementation, and design rationale. The release includes IB-HL-ET (an enriched-text version of IB-HL containing 217B o200k_base tokens across 983,003 volumes, organized into 1.39B annotated subtopic paragraphs) and the pipeline that produced it. These serve to make the collection easier for machines to parse and for humans to study.

</details>


### [152] [Intercepting the Kangaroo: Experimental Astrolinguistics with Constructed Lexicons, Active Probing, and Large Language Models as Informants and Hypothesis Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19124)
*Francesco Cordella,Mauro Cappelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将"天体语言学"（Astrolinguistics）从纯思辨变为实验科学。通过两个具有刻意不兼容词汇表的语言模型作为信息提供者，一个完全脚本化的编排器在两种范畴系统之间进行翻译。核心贡献是提出了"袋鼠效应"（kangaroo effect）作为翻译不确定性的操作化定义，并设计了一套结合多种策略的协议，在400+次实验中实现了零未检测误译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Freudenthal于1960年提出Lincos以来，天体语言学（即与范畴化现实方式不同于人类的智能体进行通信）一直停留在纯思辨层面。作者希望将这一领域推进到可实验的实证层面，并解决翻译不确定性问题（Quine的翻译不确定性理论的操作化）。

Method: 方法包含三层：1) 实验设置上，构造两个词汇表不兼容的LLM作为信息提供者（一个编码形状/颜色/运动，另一个将颜色与运动融合、编码奇偶性但不含形状），由完全脚本化的编排器进行翻译；2) 核心协议结合了交叉情境排除法、预注册预测探针、主动场景选择、更严格的恢复轮次和隔离机制；3) 对脚本化假设空间外的词汇，采用生成-测试循环（LLM提出规则，脚本验证）来恢复。

Result: 在400+次模拟和实况运行中，协议在测试条件下实现了零未检测误译，并超过被动基线的覆盖率（效应量d=0.62）。注入的袋鼠陷阱在100%运行中挫败了朴素指称学习法和纯统计学习，而完整协议拦截了所有诱饵。在信息提供者噪声条件下优雅退化：2%词级噪声下零袋鼠持续存在，10%噪声下协议主要选择弃权而非犯错。生成-测试循环的覆盖率随提议者能力提升（0%→18%→72%→100%），未检测误译始终保持为零。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，正确性是协议（protocol）的属性，而覆盖率是工具（instruments）的属性。这一结论将翻译的可靠性归因于方法论设计而非底层模型能力，为天体语言学和跨范畴通信提供了可验证的实验框架。

Abstract: Astrolinguistics -- communication with minds that categorize reality differently from ours -- has been purely speculative since Freudenthal's Lincos (1960). We make it experimental. Two language models with deliberately incompatible constructed lexicons (one encoding shape, color, and motion; the other fusing color with motion, encoding parity, and lacking shape) serve as informants with complete ground truth, while a fully scripted orchestrator translates between the two category systems. The central failure mode is the kangaroo effect: the silent attachment of a word to the wrong referent -- Quine's indeterminacy of translation, operationalized. Across 400+ simulated and live runs, a protocol combining cross-situational elimination, pre-registered predictive probes, active scene selection, a stricter recovery round, and quarantine produced no undetected mistranslations under the tested conditions and exceeded a passive baseline's coverage (d = 0.62). Injected kangaroo traps defeated naive ostension and pure statistical learning in 100% of runs, while the full protocol intercepted every decoy and, where discriminating evidence is ontologically unavailable, declared Quinean equivalence classes instead of guessing. Under informant noise it degrades gracefully: zero kangaroos persist up to 2% per-word noise; at 10% the protocol predominantly abstains rather than errs. Finally, words outside the scripted hypothesis space (a history-dependent relational term and an XOR contextual homonym) are recovered by a generate-and-test loop in which an LLM proposes rules and the script verifies them: coverage scales with proposer capability (0% -> 18% -> 72% -> 100%) while undetected mistranslations stayed at zero throughout. In the tested conditions, correctness is a property of the protocol; coverage is a property of the instruments.

</details>


### [153] [Comment-level Topic Drift Analysis in the Reddit Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19133)
*Steven Morse,Daniel Runfola,Trenton W. Ford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入的动态主题建模方法，用于在评论级别检测和量化大规模语料库中的主题漂移。通过预训练语言模型为短文本生成上下文语义嵌入，分析了2006至2022年间127亿条Reddit评论，采用无监督方法识别随时间动态演变的话题聚类。研究发现政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著的方向性漂移，而音乐和体育等领域保持相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态主题建模方法在大规模短文本语料上计算成本高且难以捕捉细粒度的语义漂移，作者希望利用预训练语言模型生成的上下文嵌入，在嵌入空间本身中分析语义漂移和话语演化，从而突破传统方法的规模限制和粒度限制。

Method: 该方法包括三个核心部分：(1) 利用预训练语言模型为短文本生成上下文语义嵌入；(2) 在嵌入上采用无监督方法识别随时间动态演化的话题聚类；(3) 对现有方法进行修改以支持超大规模分析，并提出零模型对比检验来过滤虚假动态。

Result: 研究发现政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著的方向性漂移，话题间距离随时间系统性变化，且这些变化超出了零模型所能解释的范围；而音乐和体育等领域则相对稳定。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提出了一套在嵌入空间中分析语义漂移和话语演化的方法论，证明了该方法能够在大规模语料上有效运作，并为区分真实语义漂移与虚假动态提供了统计检验框架。

Abstract: We present a novel application of embedding-based dynamic topic modeling techniques to detect and quantify topic drift at the comment level in a massive corpus. By leveraging pretrained language models to generate contextualized semantic embeddings for short text, we analyzed 12.7 billion Reddit comments spanning 2006 to 2022. Using unsupervised methods on these embeddings, we identify dynamically evolving topic clusters over time. Our primary contribution is a methodology for analysis of semantic drift and discourse evolution in the embedding space itself. We also demonstrate modifications to existing methods that enable this analysis at scale, and we propose and demonstrate a null model comparison test to filter spurious dynamics. Key findings suggest that politically and socially contentious topics exhibit significant directional drift in embedding space, with inter-topic distances changing systematically over time beyond what the null model can explain, whereas domains such as music and sports remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [154] [ChildSafeAds Shared Task 2026: Commercial Content in Child-Facing YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19165)
*Thales Bertaglia,Catalina Goanta,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gunes Acar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍ChildSafeAds共享任务，该任务旨在识别YouTube视频中可能面向儿童和青少年受众的商业内容。数据集包含939个频道的3,360个视频，每个实例来自SponsorBlock众包扩展标记的赞助片段，并配有转录文本、视频与频道信息及链接页面。任务包括三个子任务：判断推广内容类型（ST1）、分配产品类别（ST2）、识别法律风险标志（ST3），证据分为四个递进访问级别以比较数据收集成本。45.5%的视频未正确使用平台广告披露标签，GPT-5.4在专家迭代后生成标注标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童和青少年是YouTube等平台的主要受众，但面向他们的商业内容（如赞助广告）缺乏有效的识别和标注机制，且法律合规风险较高。作者希望通过构建标准化数据集和共享任务，推动对未成年人可能接触的商业内容进行自动化识别与监管。

Method: 作者从SponsorBlock众包浏览器扩展中收集用户标记的赞助片段，并将每个片段与转录文本、视频及频道信息、视频描述中链接的销售或服务页面配对。任务设置三个子任务（内容类型ST1、产品类别ST2、法律风险标志ST3），数据证据分为四个递进访问级别。使用GPT-5.4，由专家组织团队审查样本并迭代优化分类体系、提示词和模型选择来生成标注，GPT-5.6-luna独立标注开发集。

Result: 构建了包含3,360个视频、939个频道的数据集；发现45.5%的视频未正确使用平台内置广告披露方法（"包含付费推广"标签）；通过GPT-5.4和GPT-5.6-luna完成了标注工作，三层次任务及多级别数据访问设计已就绪。

Conclusion: 本文报告了ChildSafeAds任务的设计、数据和评估方法。更新版本将补充参赛系统及共享任务结果，该任务有望为未成年人商业内容监管提供标准化的评测基准。

Abstract: ChildSafeAds is a shared task on commercial content in YouTube videos likely to reach children and teenagers. It contains 3,360 videos from 939 channels. Each instance begins with a segment submitted to SponsorBlock, an open-source crowdsourced browser extension whose users mark sponsor segments so that others can skip them. We pair the segment with its available transcript, video and channel information, and a sales or service page linked from the video description. Systems determine what kind of offer is being promoted (ST1), assign product categories (ST2), and identify legal risk flags (ST3). The evidence is divided into four cumulative access levels, from the transcript to the linked page, so results can be compared against the cost of collecting the data. 45.5\% of videos in our data failed to properly use the in-platform ad disclosure method (the ``Includes paid promotion'' label). GPT-5.4 produced the labels after the expert organiser team reviewed samples and iterated on the taxonomy, prompts and model choices. GPT-5.6-luna independently labelled the development set. This report describes the task, data and evaluation. An updated version will add participating systems and shared-task results.

</details>


### [155] [SPADE: Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19197)
*Bo Liu,Simon Yu,Yiding Jiang,Ao Qu,Andrew Zhao,Zichen Liu,Junsu Kim,Zijian Zhou,Seungone Kim,Tongzheng Ren,Mickel Liu,Hanfei Yu,Zhaorun Chen,Weiyan Shi,Paul Pu Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPADE提出一种自博弈强化学习框架，让单一LLM同时扮演环境设计者和推理智能体两个角色。环境设计者编写可执行代码形式的长视野训练环境（Gym风格接口），推理智能体在其中学习。通过优化推理智能体的遗憾信号（有无特权提示的奖励差距），环境设计者学会生成处于智能体能力边缘但可行的环境。实验表明SPADE在数学、科学、代码、推理和工具使用等多个基准上超越最强固定环境基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体需要不断增加的自生成、多样化和适应性目标来实现持续自我改进，但现有训练环境池（手工策划、静态合成或冻结验证器）在学习者规模扩展时保持目标分布固定，无法实现真正的开放环境适应性。

Method: SPADE采用自博弈框架：一个LLM扮演两个角色——Environment Designer生成带有OpenAI Gym风格reset()/step()接口的可执行代码环境（覆盖状态转移、奖励函数和验证代码），Reasoning Agent在其中学习。设计者通过优化智能体有无特权提示之间的奖励差距（遗憾信号）来生成处于能力边缘的可学环境。关键组件包括：基于大型预训练语料采样文档来锚定环境设计，以及为设计者提供累积的环境记忆。

Result: 扩展到30B参数模型时，SPADE在8个保留的数学、科学、代码和推理基准上平均比最强固定环境基线高出+5.3；工具使用设置中BFCL-v4多轮提升+5.7，ACEBench-Agent提升+13.9；在游戏设置中，相对最强基线的优势随模型规模增长而扩大。

Conclusion: 通过将环境设计本身变为可学习的组件，SPADE为开放式的自我改进迈出了具体一步，使环境分布不再固定，而是随学习者的能力进步而自适应演化。

Abstract: Continuous self-improvement requires an ever-expanding pool of self-generated, diverse, adaptive goals. For language agents, existing training environment pools (hand-curated, statically synthesized, or frozen-verifier) keep the goal distribution fixed as the learner scales. We introduce SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments), a self-play RL framework in which a single LLM plays two roles: an Environment Designer that writes complete, long-horizon training environments as executable code with an OpenAI Gym-style reset()/step() interface, and a Reasoning Agent that learns to act in them. Each is a stateful, multi-turn environment (state transitions, reward functions, and verification code), so one interface spans reasoning problems and multi-step agentic tool use. The Reasoning Agent's regret is estimated using the gap between its reward with and without privileged hints; in optimizing this regret signal the Environment Designer learns to target environments at the edge of the agent's capabilities while keeping them feasible. Through extensive experimentation, we find several components critical to success: grounding the Environment Designer on documents sampled from a large pretraining corpus, and giving it an accumulated environment memory. Scaling to 30B-parameter models, SPADE improves over the strongest fixed-environment baseline by +5.3 on average across eight held-out math, science, code, and reasoning benchmarks, and lifts the tool-use setting by +5.7 on BFCL-v4 multi-turn and +13.9 on ACEBench-Agent; on the games setting, the margin over the strongest baseline grows with model scale. By making environment design itself a learnable component, SPADE takes a concrete step toward open-ended self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.PM'></div>

# q-fin.PM [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [The Market's Conditioning Representation: Equilibrium, Crowding, and Convention Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18299)
*Alejandro Rodriguez Dominguez*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个"表征均衡"框架，将资产定价模型中的固定信息集内生化，使投资组合能够选择表征形式，其暴露影响价格。该框架区分了位置拥挤和表征拥挤，分析了价格反馈与认证循环的固定点，并揭示了决定路径唯一性/多重性的谱临界值及其在数据中的可测试特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资产定价模型假设外生的固定信息集，但现实中市场参与者的信息表征方式（即投资组合的"表示"选择）会内生地影响市场定价。本文的动机在于理解信息表征的内生选择如何与资产定价相互反馈，以及这种反馈在何种条件下导致价格路径的唯一性或多重性。

Method: 构建表征均衡模型，将配置-价格-认证构成闭环不动点；区分位置拥挤（市场冲击）与表征拥挤（驱动空间重叠）；运用紧致性、预解正则性和连续认证证明均衡存在性；通过谱统计量（交叉冲击×协方差×容量）分析三种路径状态；结合最小二乘学习和中介对冲分析模型行为。

Result: 均衡在任意切换成本下存在；谱临界值1/2决定三种路径状态：低于时唯一、边界仅余无创新偏差、高于时存在连续统自证惯例。单调冲击位于唯一区域内，但非定冲击不足以保证多重性。学习机制使主导惯例趋于中性而非吸引性，风险容量约束了惯例振幅。

Conclusion: 论文从理论层面将信息表征内生化到资产定价中，区隔了两种拥挤机制，并给出了路径唯一性与多重性的精确谱条件。实证部分不直接估计临界值，而是记录与表征拥挤一致的驱动者特异性样本外特征，并阐明直接测量的前提条件。

Abstract: Asset-pricing models typically condition on a fixed information set. This paper endogenises the market's conditioning architecture by allowing portfolios to choose representations whose induced exposures affect prices. Capital allocated across representations determines aggregate positions and the clearing premium, while price feedback changes representation value and, through causal certification, admissible representations. A representation equilibrium is the fixed point of this configuration--price--certification loop. The framework separates position crowding through market impact from representation crowding generated by driver-space overlap and priced through a basis-invariant information-capacity cost. Under compactness, resolvent regularity and continuous certification, equilibrium exists at every switching cost; within a stable certification cell, informational congestion yields a concave population game and local uniqueness under a small-gain condition. At a settled configuration, a spectral statistic combining cross-impact, covariance and deployed capacity determines three position-path regimes: below one half the fundamental solution is unique; at the boundary only innovation-free deviations remain; above it, destabilising directions support a continuum of self-confirming conventions. Monotone impact lies inside the uniqueness region, while indefinite impact alone is not sufficient for multiplicity. Intermediary hedging can generate an indefinite response while round trips remain costly. Endogenous risk capacity bounds convention amplitudes, and least-squares learning makes the dominant convention neutral rather than attracting. The threshold is not estimated in data; instead, the empirical exercise documents an out-of-sample, driver-specific signature consistent with representation crowding and states the conditions required for direct measurement.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [TractoGraphVLM: A Unified Vision-Language Framework for White Matter Tractography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18166)
*Gurucharan Marthi Krishna Kumar,Janine Dale Mendola,Amir Shmuel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出TractoGraphVLM，一个统一的多任务视觉语言模型框架，用于解决3D白质纤维束的四个任务（束分类、文本-纤维束检索、解剖学描述生成和视觉问答）。该框架基于GPS图Transformer架构，将纤维束表示为流线图，通过对比学习与冻结的BiomedBERT文本编码器对齐，并使用BioGPT解码器生成描述和答案。在HCP Young Adult数据上训练，单一检查点同时完成四个任务，且能零样本迁移到HCP Aging数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型已成功应用于2D医学影像，但将其扩展到3D白质纤维束追踪领域面临挑战，因为纤维束具有复杂的拓扑结构。现有方法通常将各任务独立处理，缺乏统一的多任务框架，且未充分利用语言监督信号来学习更丰富的神经解剖学表征。

Method: 该方法将纤维束表示为流线图，节点编码3D位置和切线方向；采用GPS（General, Powerful, Scalable）图Transformer生成束嵌入，通过对比学习与冻结的BiomedBERT文本编码器对齐；使用带视觉前缀令牌的BioGPT解码器生成描述和回答。四个任务共享同一编码器和解码器，联合训练并从一个检查点评估。实验在HCP Young Adult上训练，在HCP Aging上进行零样本迁移测试。

Result: 在held-out测试集上取得：束分类准确率91.8%，检索R@1为84.7%，BLEU-4=20.1，ROUGE-L=66.8，VQA准确率66.4%。零样本迁移到HCP Aging时有适度下降（判别任务下降较小，生成任务下降较大）。语言监督比仅标签训练产生更丰富的表征，恢复出半球和纤维家族等结构信息。保留纤维方向的图表示优于体素基线，GPS架构表现最佳。

Conclusion: TractoGraphVLM证明了一个联合训练的多任务模型能够同时完成白质纤维束的分类、检索、描述和问答，且能学会可迁移的神经解剖学知识。生成的度量指标衡量的是与结构化知识库的一致性而非独立临床文本，但该框架仍展示了从单一模型中获取多任务神经解剖学能力的可行性。

Abstract: Vision language models have transformed 2D medical imaging, yet extending them to 3D white matter tractography remains challenging due to the complex topology of fiber bundles. We introduce TractoGraphVLM, a unified framework for four tasks, bundle classification, text-to-tract retrieval, anatomical captioning, and visual question answering, built on a shared GPS architecture, training procedure, and read-out design. Fiber bundles are represented as streamline graphs whose nodes encode 3D position and tangent orientation. A General, Powerful, Scalable (GPS) graph transformer produces bundle embeddings aligned with a frozen BiomedBERT text encoder via contrastive learning, while a BioGPT decoder with visual prefix tokens generates captions and answers. A single shared encoder and decoder is trained jointly across all four tasks and evaluated from one checkpoint. Trained on HCP Young Adult subjects, TractoGraphVLM achieves 91.8% bundle classification accuracy, 84.7% retrieval R@1, BLEU-4=20.1, ROUGE-L=66.8, and 66.4% VQA accuracy on a held-out test set. The same checkpoints transfer zero-shot to HCP Aging subjects, with a modest drop on discriminative tasks and a larger drop on generative tasks, showing robustness to age and acquisition shift. Language supervision yields richer representations than label-only training, recovering structure like hemisphere and fiber family, carried by captions but never given as a label. Swapping only the visual encoder, graphs preserving fiber orientation outperform volumetric baselines, with GPS giving the best balance. Generative metrics measure consistency with a structured knowledge base rather than independent clinical text; even so, TractoGraphVLM shows that classifying, retrieving, describing, and answering questions about a white matter bundle can be served by one jointly trained model that learns transferable neuroanatomy from language alone.

</details>


### [158] [QuARC-GS: Quantized Anchored Residual Coding for Compact Dynamic Scene Streaming with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18285)
*Vu Trung Nghia Nguyen,Yuchen Wang,Kyung Chul Lee,Kevin C. Zhou*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: QuARC-GS提出了一种量化感知的4D场景优化框架，用于在线动态场景重建和自由视角视频流传输，通过单帧基准帧加高度压缩的逐帧残差实现超低存储，压缩率相比最先进方法最高达11倍，同时保持重建质量和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3D场景在自由视角视频流传输中面临两大挑战：详细的场景表示需要巨大的存储空间，以及重建/渲染速度要求高。尤其是长视频场景下，实现可持续的在线流传输非常困难。

Method: QuARC-GS使用单个基准帧和高度压缩的逐帧残差表示场景，通过两种互补策略压缩残差：1) 量化感知锚点变形，抑制不重要的运动更新同时保留有意义的形变；2) 变化门控增密策略，仅在真实时间变化区域分配新高斯，消除冗余的外观更新。

Result: 在广泛使用的数据集上的实验表明，QuARC-GS在保持竞争性重建质量和训练速度的同时，逐帧存储量比最先进方法减少最多11倍。

Conclusion: QuARC-GS通过量化感知优化和变化门控增密策略，有效解决了动态场景在线自由视角视频流传输中存储开销过大的问题，实现了超高压缩与质量/速度的平衡。

Abstract: 3D scene representation techniques such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and Gaussian splatting have made substantial progress in novel view synthesis, achieving high-quality renderings from arbitrary view angles. More recently, such techniques have been extended to dynamic 3D scenes; however, achieving sustainable online free-viewpoint video (FVV) streaming remains challenging, especially for longer videos, due to significant storage demands of detailed scene representations and high reconstruction/rendering speed needs. To address these challenges, we propose Quantized Anchored Residual Coding Gaussian Streaming (QuARC-GS), a quantization-aware 4D scene optimization framework for online dynamic scene reconstruction that achieves ultra-high compression while maintaining reconstruction speed and quality. QuARC-GS represents a scene using a single canonical frame and highly compressed per-frame residuals. Specifically, we compress each residual through two complementary strategies targeting motion, appearance, and densification. We introduce quantization-aware anchor deformation, which suppresses insignificant motion updates while preserving meaningful deformations, maintaining reconstruction quality under low-storage streaming. Furthermore, we design a change-gated densification strategy that allocates new Gaussians only in regions exhibiting genuine temporal changes, effectively eliminating redundant appearance updates and reducing storage overhead. Extensive experiments on widely used datasets demonstrate that QuARC-GS enables competitive reconstruction quality and training speed while cutting per-frame storage by up to 11$\times$ compared to the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [159] [Optic Disc Segmentation in Fundus Images: From Classical Image Processing and Deformable Models to Modern AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18367)
*Buket D. Barkana*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于视盘（OD）定位与分割方法的结构化方法学综述，系统梳理了从经典图像处理方法、形变模型到当代基于人工智能（AI）方法的演变过程，并分析了各方法的假设、优势、局限及互补作用，同时总结了常用数据集和持续存在的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视盘的准确定位与分割对视网膜图像分析和青光眼评估至关重要，但由于光照变化、病理干扰、血管遮挡及边界模糊等因素，该任务仍具挑战性。作者希望通过结构化综述，帮助研究者理解不同方法代际间的核心原理延续与实现方式转变。

Method: 采用结构化文献检索与研究选择流程，识别涵盖主要方法学发展的代表性研究。综述首先总结常用眼底图像数据集，随后按主要机制对经典方法进行分类（如强度与阈值、直方图与熵分析、形态学、几何与霍夫变换、滤波与特征算子、纹理与区域方法、活动轮廓与水平集模型），并对代表性AI方法（从手工特征到Transformer分割、边界与形状感知学习、可提示分割、视网膜基础模型）进行逐一考察。

Result: 研究发现跨方法代际存在若干核心分割原则的持续存在，包括感兴趣区域定位、多尺度表征、几何与解剖约束、边界正则化，但其实现方式已从预定义算子转变为学习模块、损失函数、提示和预训练表征。综述同时识别出边界模糊、解剖变异性、域偏移和跨数据集泛化仍是持续挑战。

Conclusion: 作者总结指出，视盘分割方法经历了从经典图像处理到现代AI方法的演进，虽然底层实现发生了根本性变化，但核心分割原则保持延续。综述为未来研究指明了仍需解决的关键难题，即边界模糊、解剖变异、域偏移与跨数据集泛化问题。

Abstract: Accurate localization and segmentation of the optic disc (OD) are important for retinal image analysis and glaucoma assessment, yet remain challenging due to variations in illumination, pathology, vascular interference, and poorly defined boundaries. This structured methodological review examines the evolution of OD segmentation from classical image-processing and deformable models to contemporary artificial intelligence (AI)-based approaches. A structured literature search and study-selection process was used to identify representative studies spanning major methodological developments. The review first summarizes commonly used fundus-image datasets, then organizes classical methods by principal mechanisms, including intensity and thresholding, histogram and entropy analysis, morphology, geometric and Hough-transform methods, filtering and feature operators, texture- and region-based approaches, and active-contour and level-set models. This paper pays particular attention to the assumptions, strengths, limitations, and complementary roles of these methods in OD localization and boundary delineation. Representative AI approaches are subsequently examined to illustrate the transition from handcrafted features and explicitly defined priors to learned representations, Transformer-based segmentation, boundary- and shape-aware learning, promptable segmentation, and retinal foundation models. Across these methodological generations, several core segmentation principles persist, including region-of-interest localization, multiscale representation, geometric and anatomical constraints, and boundary regularization, although their implementation has shifted from predefined operators to learned modules, losses, prompts, and pretrained representations. The review further identifies boundary ambiguity, anatomical variability, domain shift, and cross-dataset generalization as continuing challenges.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [FinSkillBench: Evaluating AI Agents and Domain Skills for Investment Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18099)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinSkillBench，一个用于评估语言模型代理在投资管理领域技能使用能力的基准测试套件，涵盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三个领域共12个子任务、2,603个任务片段。实验发现：策展技能包能稳定提升性能（平均分从0.366提升至0.528），而自生成技能效果甚微且计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资管理是高风险的领域，AI代理系统不能仅生成合理的文本，还需要检索时点数据、组装正确的计算输入、调用专业方法并产生可审计的结构化输出。现有评估缺乏对金融领域技能使用能力的系统评测，因此需要一个专门的基准来衡量语言模型代理在投资管理任务中有效使用金融领域技能的能力。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench评估套件，包含投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三个领域共12个子任务、2,603个任务片段。每个任务片段提供时点输入、隐藏的真实答案和任务特定的验证器。比较三种条件：无技能、策展技能包（由程序化文档和可执行组件组成）以及自生成技能（代理在任务片段内自行编写和复用程序）。在9个模型上开展大规模评估，并使用独立的Hermes Agent框架（8个模型、5,280个片段）进行复现验证。

Result: 策展技能包一致性地提升所有模型的性能，将平均得分从0.366提升至0.528，其中投资组合构建和风险管理领域的提升最为显著。相比之下，自生成技能几乎不带来性能提升，却产生更高的计算成本。使用独立代理框架的评估在三个领域中再现了相同的趋势方向，但技能效果的幅度因子任务和测试框架而有所不同。

Conclusion: 在投资管理代理中，获取可靠的程序化技能与模型选择同等重要，而天真的自生成技能方法往往无效。研究团队发布了基准测试、评估工具、策展技能包和完整的运行轨迹以支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Investment management is a high-stakes domain in which agentic AI systems must do more than generate plausible text. They must retrieve point-in-time data, assemble correct computational inputs, invoke specialized methods, and produce auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite designed to measure whether language model agents can effectively use financial domain skills to solve investment management tasks. The benchmark spans three domains, portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, and includes 12 subtasks with 2,603 task episodes.
  Each episode provides point-in-time inputs, hidden ground truth, and a task-specific verifier.We compare three conditions: no skill, curated skill packages consisting of procedural documents and executable components, and self-generated skills in which the agent writes and reuses its own procedures within an episode. Across 9 models and a large-scale evaluation, curated skills consistently improve performance, raising mean scores from 0.366 to 0.528, with the largest gains in portfolio construction and risk management.
  In contrast, self-generated skills provide little benefit despite higher computational cost. An independent evaluation using a separate agent framework (Hermes Agent, 8 models, 5,280 episodes total) reproduces the directional pattern across all three domains, with the magnitude of skill effects varying by subtask and harness.
  These results showthat in investment management agents, access to reliable procedural skills can be as important as model choice, while naive self-generation of skills is often ineffective. We release the benchmark, evaluation tools, curated skill packages, and full trajectories to support further research.

</details>


### [161] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了具备思维链推理能力的AI智能体倾向于产生合谋行为，并主张在进入经济市场决策前需获得行为认证。实验表明DeepSeek-R1在Bertrand寡头定价中表现出默契合谋倾向，且思维链可被引导至极端合谋或竞争行为而无法被检测，因此基于行为的认证是防止合谋的必要手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将具备思维链推理能力的AI智能体融入社会可能瓦解法律上区分竞争与合谋的证据标准，同时保留经济危害层面的区分，这为经济市场带来潜在的严重合谋风险。

Method: 在Bertrand寡头定价博弈场景中对DeepSeek-R1智能体进行实验，测试其在人类提示不要合谋时的行为倾向，并通过另一LLM分析思维链轨迹来检测语义可察觉性，同时尝试将智能体引导至竞争均衡。

Result: 实验发现推理智能体表现出持续的默契合谋倾向，即使被明确告知不要合谋也无法消除；思维链可被静默引导至极端合谋或竞争行为，且这种引导无法被另一LLM从推理轨迹中语义检测到。初步证据表明可将智能体引导至有效的竞争均衡。

Conclusion: 部署推理型AI智能体参与市场决策会在无任何共谋证据或意图的情况下导致合谋性经济后果，因此需要基于代表性情境中观察行为的行为认证来防止合谋，在开发全面的行为认证框架前不应将这些模型部署至真实市场。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [162] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出过渡复杂度剖面（TCP）指标集，用于量化游戏世界建模和强化学习任务中环境转移内核的预测难度，包含单步分支、交互不确定性和时空依赖跨度三个维度，并呼吁将其作为基准元数据和论文必需统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏世界建模（GWM）与强化学习（RL）常被混淆，因为研究论文很少量化在声明接口（像素/令牌/潜在变量及有限历史）下底层转移预测问题的难度，导致跨基准无法公平比较。

Method: 提出TCP指标集，包含三个测量维度：(i)内在单步分支度，(ii)交互诱导的不确定性与可观测时的对手影响，(iii)通过标准化探针曲线测量的时空依赖跨度。TCP附带显式参考分布、协议随机性和版本化测量预算（采样/重采样及固定探针计算量）。

Result: 概述了常见游戏家族和现代"神经游戏引擎"领域如何分布在该指标景观中，论证了TCP作为基准元数据的可行性和可比性。

Conclusion: 呼吁TCP成为标准基准元数据，并作为GWM和RL论文中的必需统计量，以更好地区分环境自身难度与算法能力。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [163] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLMs）在心理健康领域的应用，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持工具、提示工程、多模态学习及伦理考量等方面，并探讨了其在抑郁症早期检测、自杀风险评估和个性化治疗支持中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问题日益严峻，传统诊断和治疗手段存在局限性，而大语言模型在自然语言处理和跨模态数据分析方面展现出巨大潜力，亟需系统性的综述来整合跨学科研究成果，推动LLMs在心理健康领域的安全、公平和负责任的部署。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，整合了来自跨学科研究的结果，利用社交媒体帖子、电子病历和多模态输入等多样化数据源，系统梳理了LLMs在心理健康检测、评估和干预中的技术进展，包括模型架构、标注策略、提示工程和多模态融合技术。

Result: 综述发现LLMs在抑郁症早期检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持和心理教育内容生成方面取得了显著进展，多模态融合技术（文本、语音和传感器数据）为心理健康诊断和监测提供了新路径。

Conclusion: 虽然LLMs在心理健康领域展现了广阔应用前景，但必须解决伦理、社会技术和监管方面的挑战，建立相关框架以确保其在真实世界心理健康护理中安全、公平和负责任地部署。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [164] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张人工智能体应像其他行为系统一样被评估，强调通过系统观察、扰动和解释其行为过程（而非仅关注性能结果）来建立"AI行为科学"，并提出包括决策策略恢复、环境构建和多智能体动力学探测在内的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体评估方法主要关注性能产出（performance outcomes），忽略了产生这些结果的行为过程。随着AI智能体日益作为行为系统与动态环境交互、追求目标并随时间适应，现有的结果导向评估无法全面理解其行为特征，亟需借鉴行为科学的方法论来改进评估范式。

Method: 论文从行为科学中汲取经验教训，提出将AI智能体作为行为系统进行评估的研究议程，包括：（1）从行动序列中恢复决策策略的方法；（2）构建能够隔离行为差异的实验环境；（3）探测多智能体系统中的涌现动力学。

Result: 本文提出了一幅发展"AI行为科学"的研究路线图，为未来的行为评估方法奠定了概念基础和方向指引。

Conclusion: AI智能体的评估需要从单纯的性能结果导向转向行为过程导向，通过系统观察、扰动和解释来理解其行为机制，最终建立一门系统的AI行为科学，从而更全面地评估和引导AI系统的行为。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [165] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析Hugging Face上500个模型卡片，提出开源权重基础模型的有效治理需要模型卡片、可接受使用政策和许可证三者协同的多层方法，并指出现有监管框架存在安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重基础模型的快速发展促使AI社区重新评估下游治理策略，但现有的模型卡片框架未能充分告知下游开发者和用户关于这些模型特有的安全挑战。

Method: 对Hugging Face上500个模型卡片进行系统分析，识别现有监管方法在模型血统、对齐溯源和实证观测行为等方面的安全缺口，并论证标准开源许可证不适用于开源权重基础模型。

Result: 发现现有模型卡片框架存在安全信息缺口，标准开源许可证可能削弱可接受使用政策的可执行性，证明需要多层次治理框架。

Conclusion: 主张将模型卡片、可接受使用政策和许可证演化为综合安全工件，构建整合信息、规范和法律维度的更全面治理框架。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [166] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于飞行日志的无人机螺旋桨健康监测决策支持原型系统，采用Metamorphic人工年龄评分（AAS）框架，通过六个健康指标综合评估螺旋桨故障，并在2024年DronePropA公开数据集上进行回顾性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障的影响可能分布在多个飞行日志通道中，而非表现为单一诊断信号，导致传统单指标监测方法难以有效识别故障，存在安全与可靠性隐患。

Method: 从真实飞行日志中计算六个健康指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定性、推力命令负担、电机命令不平衡、ESC命令不稳定性和电池电量压力），将其相对于健康基线进行归一化处理，并通过候选评分策略、Metamorphic充分性关系和冗余调整AAS公式进行综合评估。

Result: 在健康基线和三个不同严重程度的螺旋桨故障案例的回顾性评估中，健康案例被分配常规监测，严重度1由ESC命令不稳定性主导并分配维护审查，严重度2达到最大电机命令和ESC命令负担，严重度3达到最大轨迹跟踪误差，两者均触发强制检查。

Conclusion: 螺旋桨故障效应可通过不同操作通道表现，需要多指标决策支持层用于飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监督。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [167] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体系统（MAS）中的许多失败本质上是并发控制问题：智能体并发读写共享状态，LLM推理的长延迟放大了脏读、丢失更新和不一致结果的风险。作者主张MAS框架应将并发控制（冲突检测、隔离保证、共享资源的结构化访问）作为一等设计关注点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在增加智能体数量时可靠性下降，而现有的失败模式常被归因于协调或通信问题。作者认为这些失败实际上可以映射到经典的并发异常上，需要重新审视问题根源。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过将MAS中的失败模式映射到经典并发控制异常（脏读、丢失更新、不一致结果），论证并发控制机制的必要性，并提出三大核心机制：冲突检测、隔离保证和共享资源结构化访问。

Result: 论文提出MAS框架应内置显式的并发控制机制，将并发控制从"事后补救"提升为"一等公民"设计关注点，而非常规流程的附加项。

Conclusion: 并发控制应成为多智能体系统设计中的首要考量，而非事后思考；通过显式处理并发问题可以提升MAS的可扩展性和可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies. We contend that MAS frameworks should address these failures through explicit concurrency control mechanisms: conflict detection, isolation guarantees, and structured access to shared resources. Concurrency control should be a first-class design concern, not an afterthought.

</details>


### [168] [Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18104)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Wenjie Zhang,Yin Chen,Xuemin Lin,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体演化视为动态图变换的框架，将智能体状态建模为动态图（记忆、工具、技能、工作流等表示为类型化节点、边和子图），并基于此提出四种演化分类法（节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活、跨组件协同演化），建立动态图学习与智能体演化能力之间的映射，并讨论图感知的评估与治理协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图智能体综述将图视为智能体功能的支撑结构而非演化底物，而自演化智能体综述关注智能体层机制却很少讨论图拓扑演化。因此，智能体状态演化与动态图拓扑之间的耦合关系尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将智能体状态建模为动态图，通过模式约束重写更新类型化节点、边和子图；基于该形式化提出四种演化分类法（节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活、跨组件协同演化）；将九个动态图学习子领域映射到智能体演化能力，并讨论适配与失败模式。

Result: 提出了一个统一的动态图视角来连接智能体演化和图拓扑演化两条研究线，建立了动态图学习作为自演化智能体可复用基础设施的框架，并提出了五种图感知的评估与治理协议。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为设计和治理自演化智能体提供一个紧凑的结构化视角，弥补智能体状态演化与动态图拓扑之间研究的空白，推动动态图学习作为自演化智能体的基础设施。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly becoming self-evolving systems that persist across interactions, maintain memories, use tools, acquire skills, refine workflows, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make agent states structural and dynamic: entities, relations, attributes, dependencies, and execution structures change with new evidence, feedback, and environmental conditions. Existing graph-agent surveys typically treat graphs as support structures for agent functions rather than as evolving substrates, while self-evolving-agent surveys focus on agent-level mechanisms and rarely discuss graph topology evolution. Thus, the coupling between evolving agent state and dynamic graph topology remains underexplored. This survey connects these two research lines by framing \textit{agent evolution as dynamic graph transformation}. We model agent state as a dynamic graph, where memories, tools, skills, workflows, and inter-agent relations are represented as typed nodes, edges, and subgraphs updated through schema-constrained rewrites. Based on this formulation, we organize existing dynamic-graph-based methods for self-evolving agents into four taxonomies: node/feature evolution, edge/topology evolution, subgraph activation, and cross-component co-evolution. Building on this taxonomy, we propose dynamic graph learning as reusable infrastructure for self-evolving agents and map nine dynamic-graph-learning subfields to agent-evolution capabilities, discussing their adaptations and possible failure modes. Finally, we discuss five types of graph-aware evaluation and governance protocols from a dynamic-graph perspective, which complement end-task evaluation. The goal is to provide a compact structural lens for designing and governing self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [169] [Emergence of Agentic AI: A Review on Evolution, Background, Working Principles, Applications, Adoption Factors, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18110)
*AKM Bahalul Haque,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Mohammad Rayhan,Ivan Porres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Agentic AI（智能体人工智能）进行了系统性综述，涵盖其基础原理、历史演化、架构功能、跨领域应用，并提出了一个包含系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳意图框架，识别了当前研究空白并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI在人工智能领域取得了快速进展，具有变革多个领域的巨大潜力，但学术界和产业界对其缺乏深入理解和系统把握，亟需一篇综合性的综述来评估当前技术现状与未来改进和应用空间。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，收集近期发表的Agentic AI学术文献，对其跨领域研究成果进行分析与综合，涵盖理论演化梳理、架构与工作机制探讨、应用场景归类，并基于系统质量维度提出利益相关者采纳意图框架。

Result: 系统梳理了Agentic AI的基本原理与工作机制，总结了其在各领域的实际应用情况，识别了当前研究中的关键空白与挑战，并提出了一套包含系统质量维度在内的Agentic AI采纳与使用意图的综合框架。

Conclusion: 该综述为研究人员和实践者提供了对Agentic AI的全方位理解，明确了当前发展状态与应用现状，指出了关键研究缺口，并为未来研究方向提供了有价值的指引。

Abstract: Agentic AI is gaining new insights and advancements in the field of Artificial Intelligence, fostering significant potential to enable rapid transformation across various domains.This rapid advancement and the potential to revolutionize various domains advocate the need for a deeper understanding and firm grasp of the technology. Moreover, an investigation into state of the art research directions in agentic AI needs to be conducted to comprehensively assess the potential scope for improvement and application.Therefore, to address these objectives, a comprehensive review can provide researchers and practitioners with valuable insights into the current state and future research scopes of agentic AI.Hence, this work considers the recently published scholarly contributions in agentic AI across various domains and discusses the fundamentals and working principles of Agentic AI, traces the historical and theoretical evolution of agency in artificial systems, explores and discusses Agentic AIs architecture, working principles, and functionalities, explores real-world applications of Agentic AI across various domains, analyzes the research findings, identifies current challenges, and discuss potential future research directions, and proposes a comprehensive framework of stakeholders intention to use and adopt Agentic AI with the help of proposed system quality dimensions.Therefore, this systematic review provides researchers and practitioners with a comprehensive understanding of Agentic AI, its current developments and applications, highlights key research gaps, and outlines future research directions.

</details>


### [170] [Solving Is Not Drawing: A Benchmark for Diagrammatic Reasoning in Olympiad Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18111)
*Hsien Xin Peng,Anthony Kim,Alvin Li,Calvin Supasanya,Shivank Garg,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源基准测试，包含954道奥林匹克几何问题（其中297道为困难子集），每道题配有解决方案和人类撰写的高保真Asymptote代码图表，并提供了多种度量指标来评估模型的"图表推理"能力。实验发现，当前基础模型在解题与绘图之间存在显著差距，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%，表明强数学推理能力并不等同于能够构造准确的几何图形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试（如MathVista、MathVerse）仅衡量模型能否得出正确答案，但没有一个基准专门隔离并测量模型构造几何图表本身的能力。解决几何问题和绘制其所依赖的图形是两种不同技能，而后者在很大程度上未被测量。

Method: 构建了一个包含954道自包含奥林匹克几何问题的开源基准测试，每道题配有解决方案和人类编写的高保真可渲染Asymptote代码图表。引入了一套基于文本、代码、图像、VLM和约束的度量指标来评估"图表推理"能力。

Result: 评估当前基础模型后发现解题与绘图之间存在显著差距：模型生成的图表保真度明显不足，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%，表明强数学推理能力并不等同于能构造准确的几何图形。

Conclusion: 基础模型虽然能解决奥林匹克级别的几何问题，但在绘制忠实图表方面表现不佳，揭示了一个未被现有基准覆盖的能力缺口。该基准和数据集已公开，供研究社区使用。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude now solve olympiad-level mathematics with remarkable proficiency, so much so that geometry problem solving has become a standard proxy for their mathematical reasoning. Yet solving a geometry problem and drawing the figure it depends on are not the same skill: progress often hinges on a faithful diagram with the right auxiliary constructions and incidences, and it is unclear that a model which reasons its way to the answer can also produce one. A growing collection of benchmarks, including MathVista, and MathVerse, measures whether models reach the correct answer, but to our knowledge, none isolate the distinct ability to construct the diagram itself, leaving this capability unmeasured. We introduce an open-source benchmark that targets this gap: 954 self-contained olympiad geometry problems, with a 297-problem hard subset, each paired with its solution and a human-authored, high-fidelity diagram in renderable Asymptote code, together with a suite of text-, code-, image-, VLM-, and constraint-based metrics for what we term diagrammatic reasoning. Evaluating current foundation models reveals a pronounced gap between solving and drawing: their diagrams are markedly less faithful, with an average compile success rate of only 36.14\%. Strong mathematical reasoning, we find, does not imply the ability to construct accurate geometric diagrams. Our benchmark and dataset can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/max98765/hard_geometry_problems_with_diagrams.

</details>


### [171] [Position: AI Leaderboards Are Underserving the Global South: A Case Study from India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18117)
*Sourav Banerjee,Saikat Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为AI排行榜在制度设计上不利于全球南方国家，主要问题在于缺乏独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演进机制。以印度为案例（14亿人口、22种法定语言），通过对58名AI从业者的咨询调研，提出解决方案不是更多数据，而是建立具有独立治理的区域性排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI排行榜在制度设计层面系统性忽视了全球南方国家（如印度、非洲、阿拉伯地区），即使高质量的区域基准已存在（如IndicSUPERB、MILU、IrokoBench等），也没有机制迫使全球排行榜纳入这些基准。商业压力只对北方付费客户有效，南方缺乏同等杠杆，导致问题长期无法解决。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，以印度为案例研究，调研了58名AI从业者的意见，分析现有全球AI排行榜在独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演进机制三个维度的结构性缺陷。

Result: 调研显示，58名AI从业者一致倾向于正式治理结构和基于披露的冲突管理机制。现有高质量区域基准（印度的IndicSUPERB、MILU、LAHAJA，非洲的IrokoBench，阿拉伯的AlGhafa）虽然存在，但未被全球排行榜采纳。

Conclusion: 解决问题的关键不是收集更多数据，而是建设更好的制度——从一开始就应建立具有独立治理的区域性排行榜，而非依赖全球排行榜的自我修正。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI leaderboards are structurally ill-suited to serving the Global South because they lack independent governance, conflict-of-interest policies, and mechanisms for metric evolution. The barrier is not missing data; high-quality regional benchmarks already exist: IndicSUPERB, MILU, and LAHAJA for India; IrokoBench for Africa; AlGhafa for Arabic. The barrier is institutional design. Global leaderboards do not include these benchmarks, and no governance mechanism compels them to do so. Commercial pressure corrects leaderboard failures when paying customers in the Global North are affected. The Global South lacks equivalent leverage. Without governance, failures affecting Hindi, Swahili, or Arabic speakers persist indefinitely as documented but unaddressed gaps. Using India as a case study (1.4 billion people, 22 scheduled languages, high-quality benchmarks, but no trusted aggregation), we report findings from a consultation with 58 AI practitioners showing consistent preference for formal governance and disclosure-based conflict management. The solution is not more data but better institutions: regional leaderboards with independent governance from the start.

</details>


### [172] [Safety Alignment Illusion: The Cross-Lingual Safety Gap in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18131)
*Namya Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了INCLUDE，一个用于评估多语言大语言模型在印度社会文化偏见方面表现的基准测试集，包含2,604个提示和六种语言，通过对十个开源与闭源模型的分析发现了显著的语言间偏见差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐训练主要针对英语，导致非英语语言场景中安全过滤器失效，可能产生强化刻板印象的输出并对非英语社区传播有害偏见。印度拥有语言多样性极高的人口，这一跨语言安全缺口尤为严重。

Method: 构建INCLUDE基准，涵盖英语、印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语、泰米尔语和Hinglish六种语言（共2,604个提示），评估十个开源和闭源LLM，分析14,988个偏见分数。

Result: 统计结果显示两个关键发现：第一，孟加拉语在开源模型中产生最高的平均偏见分数；第二，英语呈现显著反转——在开源模型中偏见最低，但在闭源模型中偏见最高。

Conclusion: 不同语言和不同模型类型之间的偏见表现存在显著差异，这凸显了在LLM安全对齐中需要更多关注非英语语言的跨语言偏见检测与缓解。

Abstract: Current safety alignment training for Large Language Models (LLMs) are heavily English-centric.
